          </p>

            <li class="flex items-start gap-2"><i data-lucide="chevron-right" class="w-5 h-5 text-rose-500 shrink-0 mt-0.5"></i> <span>It moves much faster than the short hour hand.</span></li>
            <li class="flex items-start gap-2"><i data-lucide="chevron-right" class="w-5 h-5 text-rose-500 shrink-0 mt-0.5"></i> <span>When it points straight UP at <strong>12</strong>, it means <strong>00 minutes</strong> (O'clock!).</span></li>
            <li class="flex items-start gap-2"><i data-lucide="chevron-right" class="w-5 h-5 text-rose-500 shrink-0 mt-0.5"></i> <span>When it points straight DOWN at <strong>6</strong>, it means <strong>30 minutes</strong> (Half-past!).</span></li>

  <!-- CORE JAVASCRIPT BLOCK (IIFE WRAPPED FOR SAFETY & TO PREVENT REDECLARATION CRASHES) -->
  <script>
  (function() {
    // 1. STATE VARIABLES
    let voiceEnabled = true;
    let sandboxHour = 12;
    let sandboxMinute = 0;
    let sandboxPeriod = "PM";

    // Quiz State
    let quizCurrentIndex = 0;
    let quizScore = 0;
    const TOTAL_QUIZ_QUESTIONS = 20;

    // AUDIO SYNTHESIS SYSTEM (Web Audio API)
    // Create organic, synthesized sound effects safely without relying on external assets
    let audioCtx = null;
    function initAudioContext() {
      if (!audioCtx) {
        audioCtx = new (window.AudioContext || window.webkitAudioContext)();
      }
    }

    function playSound(type) {
      try {
        initAudioContext();
        if (audioCtx.state === 'suspended') {
          audioCtx.resume();
        }
        
        const osc = audioCtx.createOscillator();
        const gainNode = audioCtx.createGain();
        osc.connect(gainNode);
        gainNode.connect(audioCtx.destination);

        const now = audioCtx.currentTime;

        if (type === 'correct') {
          // Playful happy arpeggio
          osc.type = 'triangle';
          osc.frequency.setValueAtTime(523.25, now); // C5
          osc.frequency.setValueAtTime(659.25, now + 0.1); // E5
          osc.frequency.setValueAtTime(783.99, now + 0.2); // G5
          osc.frequency.setValueAtTime(1046.50, now + 0.3); // C6
          gainNode.gain.setValueAtTime(0.15, now);
          gainNode.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.01, now + 0.5);
          osc.start(now);
          osc.stop(now + 0.5);
        } else if (type === 'incorrect') {
          // Low gentle buzz
          osc.type = 'sawtooth';
          osc.frequency.setValueAtTime(150, now);
          osc.frequency.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(80, now + 0.3);
          gainNode.gain.setValueAtTime(0.12, now);
          gainNode.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.01, now + 0.3);
          osc.start(now);
          osc.stop(now + 0.3);
        } else if (type === 'click') {
          // Soft pop
          osc.type = 'sine';
          osc.frequency.setValueAtTime(400, now);
          osc.frequency.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(100, now + 0.08);
          gainNode.gain.setValueAtTime(0.2, now);
          gainNode.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.01, now + 0.08);
          osc.start(now);
          osc.stop(now + 0.08);
        } else if (type === 'success') {
          // Grand fanfare
          osc.type = 'sine';
          osc.frequency.setValueAtTime(261.63, now); // C4
          osc.frequency.setValueAtTime(329.63, now + 0.15); // E4
          osc.frequency.setValueAtTime(392.00, now + 0.3); // G4
          osc.frequency.setValueAtTime(523.25, now + 0.45); // C5
          gainNode.gain.setValueAtTime(0.2, now);
          gainNode.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.01, now + 0.8);
          osc.start(now);
          osc.stop(now + 0.8);
        }
      } catch (err) {
        console.warn("Web Audio API blocked or unsupported:", err);
      }
    }

    // 2. SPEECH SYNTHESIS ASSISTANT
    window.speak = function(text) {
      if (!voiceEnabled) return;
      try {
        window.speechSynthesis.cancel(); // Stop current speaking
        const utterance = new SpeechSynthesisUtterance(text);
        utterance.rate = 0.85; // Slower, clear rate for child comprehension
        utterance.pitch = 1.25; // Warm, friendly, higher pitch tone
        window.speechSynthesis.speak(utterance);
      } catch (err) {
        console.error("Speech Synthesis failed:", err);
      }
    };

    window.toggleVoice = function() {
      voiceEnabled = !voiceEnabled;
      const btn = document.getElementById("voiceToggleBtn");
      const icon = document.getElementById("voiceIcon");
      const statusText = document.getElementById("voiceStatusText");
      
      if (voiceEnabled) {
        btn.className = "flex items-center gap-2 px-4 py-2 bg-honey-100 hover:bg-honey-200 border-2 border-honey-500 rounded-full text-honey-800 font-bold text-sm transition-all transform hover:scale-105";
        icon.setAttribute("data-lucide", "volume-2");
        statusText.textContent = "Voice: ON";
        window.speak("Voice is now on! Let's tell time together!");
      } else {
        btn.className = "flex items-center gap-2 px-4 py-2 bg-slate-100 hover:bg-slate-200 border-2 border-slate-400 rounded-full text-slate-600 font-bold text-sm transition-all transform hover:scale-105";
        icon.setAttribute("data-lucide", "volume-x");
        statusText.textContent = "Voice: OFF";
        window.speechSynthesis.cancel();
      }
      lucide.createIcons();
      playSound('click');
    };

    window.speakWelcome = function() {
      window.speak("Bzzz! Hello there, little explorer! Welcome to my Clock Academy. Let's learn how to read watches together. Click the learning tabs below to begin the adventure!");
    };

    // Lesson specific speak text
    window.speakLesson = function(lessonKey) {
      let text = "";
      if (lessonKey === 'anatomy') {
        text = "Secret number one! Meet the clock face. The clock is like a round neighborhood with 12 houses on it. The numbers go in a big circle from 1 all the way to 12. The top house is always 12, and the bottom house is always 6!";
      } else if (lessonKey === 'hours') {
        text = "Secret number two! The short hour hand. The clock has two main pointers called hands. The short, thick blue hand is the Hour Hand. Whichever number it points to tells us the hour! If it points right at 3, it is 3 o'clock!";
      } else if (lessonKey === 'minutes') {
        text = "Secret number three! The long minute hand. The long, tall red hand is the Minute Hand. It measures the tiny, fast minutes! When it points straight up at 12, it means zero-zero minutes, or o'clock. When it points straight down at 6, it means 30 minutes, or half-past!";
      } else if (lessonKey === 'secrets') {
        text = "Secret number four! Magic time numbers! Time has a magical pattern. Sixty seconds makes one minute. Sixty minutes makes one whole hour. And twenty-four hours makes one full day of fun!";
      }
      window.speak(text);
    };

    // 3. INTERACTIVE TAB SYSTEM (classList based)
    window.showTab = function(id, btn) {
      playSound('click');
      document.querySelectorAll('.tab-panel').forEach(p => p.classList.remove('active'));
      document.querySelectorAll('.tab-btn').forEach(b => {
        b.className = "tab-btn px-6 py-3 bg-slate-100 border-2 border-transparent text-slate-600 font-heading font-black text-base rounded-2xl flex items-center gap-2 transition-all";
      });
      
      const panel = document.getElementById(id);
      if (panel) panel.classList.add('active');
      if (btn) {
        btn.className = "tab-btn active px-6 py-3 bg-honey-100 border-2 border-honey-400 text-slate-800 font-heading font-black text-base rounded-2xl flex items-center gap-2 transition-all";
      }
    };

    // 4. ANALOG CLOCK MECHANICS & GENERATOR
    function generateMinuteTicks() {
      const group = document.getElementById("minuteTicks");
      if (!group) return;
      group.innerHTML = "";
      for (let i = 0; i < 60; i++) {
        if (i % 5 === 0) continue; // Skip numbers
        const angle = (i * 6) * Math.PI / 180;
        const x1 = 150 + 115 * Math.sin(angle);
        const y1 = 150 - 115 * Math.cos(angle);
        const x2 = 150 + 120 * Math.sin(angle);
        const y2 = 150 - 120 * Math.cos(angle);
        
        const line = document.createElementNS("http://www.w3.org/2000/svg", "line");
        line.setAttribute("x1", x1);
        line.setAttribute("y1", y1);
        line.setAttribute("x2", x2);
        line.setAttribute("y2", y2);
        group.appendChild(line);
      }
    }

    function updateClockHands(hour, minute, hourHandId, minuteHandId) {
      const hHand = document.getElementById(hourHandId);
      const mHand = document.getElementById(minuteHandId);
      if (!hHand || !mHand) return;

      // Minute hand angle: 360 degrees / 60 minutes = 6 degrees per minute
      const mAngle = minute * 6;
      // Hour hand angle: 30 degrees per hour + extra based on minutes (30 / 60 = 0.5 degrees per minute)
      const hAngle = (hour % 12) * 30 + minute * 0.5;

      hHand.setAttribute("transform", `rotate(${hAngle})`);
      mHand.setAttribute("transform", `rotate(${mAngle})`);
    }

    // Adjust Sandbox Time
    window.adjustTime = function(hDelta, mDelta) {
      playSound('click');
      sandboxHour += hDelta;
      sandboxMinute += mDelta;

      // Handle minute overflow
      if (sandboxMinute >= 60) {
        sandboxMinute = 0;
        sandboxHour += 1;
      } else if (sandboxMinute < 0) {
        sandboxMinute = 55;
        sandboxHour -= 1;
      }

      // Handle hour overflow
      if (sandboxHour > 12) {
        sandboxHour = 1;
        sandboxPeriod = (sandboxPeriod === "AM") ? "PM" : "AM";
      } else if (sandboxHour < 1) {
        sandboxHour = 12;
        sandboxPeriod = (sandboxPeriod === "AM") ? "PM" : "AM";
      }

      // Update Sandbox UI
      document.getElementById("digitalHour").textContent = sandboxHour;
      document.getElementById("digitalMinute").textContent = String(sandboxMinute).padStart(2, '0');
      document.getElementById("digitalPeriod").textContent = sandboxPeriod;

      updateClockHands(sandboxHour, sandboxMinute, "hourHand", "minuteHand");
      updateDayNightTheme(sandboxHour, sandboxPeriod);
    };

    function updateDayNightTheme(hour, period) {
      const bg = document.getElementById("clockBackground");
      const indicator = document.getElementById("skyIndicator");
      if (!bg || !indicator) return;

      const isNight = (hour >= 7 && hour < 12 && period === "PM") || (hour === 12 && period === "AM") || (hour < 6 && period === "AM");
      
      if (isNight) {
        bg.style.background = "linear-gradient(135deg, #1e1b4b 0%, #312e81 100%)";
        indicator.textContent = "🌙";
      } else {
        bg.style.background = "linear-gradient(135deg, #fef08a 0%, #fef3c7 100%)";
        indicator.textContent = "☀️";
      }
    }

    window.speakCurrentSandboxTime = function() {
      let minText = "";
      if (sandboxMinute === 0) {
        minText = "o'clock";
      } else if (sandboxMinute === 30) {
        minText = "thirty";
      } else {
        minText = sandboxMinute;
      }
      window.speak(`The sandbox clock is set to ${sandboxHour} ${minText} ${sandboxPeriod}!`);
    };

    // 5. 20-QUESTION ADVENTURE QUIZ ENGINE
    // Comprehensive array of exactly 20 kid-friendly questions covering hours, halves, quarters, and specific minutes
    const quizQuestions = [
      { h: 1, m: 0, options: ["1:00", "12:00", "2:00", "6:00"], answer: "1:00", type: "read", text: "Look at the clock! What time is it when the short hand is on 1 and the long hand is on 12?" },

      { h: 5, m: 55, options: ["5:55", "5:11", "6:55", "5:30"], answer: "5:55", type: "read", text: "Our final question! The clock is almost 6:00. It is 5:55!" }
    ];

    window.startQuiz = function() {
      playSound('click');
      quizCurrentIndex = 0;
      quizScore = 0;
      
      document.getElementById("quizStartScreen").classList.add("hidden");
      document.getElementById("quizResultScreen").classList.add("hidden");
      document.getElementById("quizQuestionScreen").classList.remove("hidden");
      document.getElementById("quizQuestionScreen").classList.add("flex");

      buildQuizProgressDots();
      loadQuizQuestion();
    };

    function buildQuizProgressDots() {
      const container = document.getElementById("quizDotsContainer");
      if (!container) return;
      container.innerHTML = "";
      for (let i = 0; i < TOTAL_QUIZ_QUESTIONS; i++) {
        const dot = document.createElement("div");
        dot.className = "w-2 h-2 rounded-full bg-sky-300 transition-all";
        dot.id = `quizDot-${i}`;
        container.appendChild(dot);
      }
    }

    function loadQuizQuestion() {
      const q = quizQuestions[quizCurrentIndex];
      
      // Update progress text
      document.getElementById("quizCurrentNum").textContent = quizCurrentIndex + 1;
      document.getElementById("quizScoreText").textContent = `Stars: ${quizScore}`;

      // Highlight active dot
      for (let i = 0; i < TOTAL_QUIZ_QUESTIONS; i++) {
        const dot = document.getElementById(`quizDot-${i}`);
        if (dot) {
          if (i === quizCurrentIndex) {
            dot.className = "w-3 h-3 rounded-full bg-sky-600 ring-2 ring-white scale-125 transition-all";
          } else if (i < quizCurrentIndex) {
            dot.className = "w-2 h-2 rounded-full bg-garden-500 transition-all"; // green for completed
          } else {
            dot.className = "w-2 h-2 rounded-full bg-sky-300 transition-all";
          }
        }
      }

      // Set Question Title
      document.getElementById("quizQuestionTitle").textContent = q.text;

      // Update Quiz Clock Visuals
      updateClockHands(q.h, q.m, "quizHourHand", "quizMinuteHand");

      // Generate Option Buttons
      const container = document.getElementById("quizAnswersContainer");
      container.innerHTML = "";
      q.options.forEach(opt => {
        const btn = document.createElement("button");
        btn.className = "w-full py-3 px-6 bg-slate-100 hover:bg-sky-100 border-2 border-slate-300 hover:border-sky-400 font-heading font-black text-lg text-slate-700 rounded-2xl transition-all transform active:scale-95";
        btn.textContent = opt;
        btn.onclick = () => handleAnswerSubmit(opt, btn);
        container.appendChild(btn);
      });

      // Auto-read question if voice is enabled
      setTimeout(() => {
        window.speak(q.text);
      }, 300);
    }

    window.speakQuizQuestion = function() {
      const q = quizQuestions[quizCurrentIndex];
      window.speak(q.text);
    };

    function handleAnswerSubmit(selectedOption, clickedBtn) {
      const q = quizQuestions[quizCurrentIndex];
      const isCorrect = selectedOption === q.answer;

      // Disable all buttons in container
      const container = document.getElementById("quizAnswersContainer");
      const buttons = container.querySelectorAll("button");
      buttons.forEach(btn => btn.disabled = true);

      if (isCorrect) {
        quizScore++;
        playSound('correct');
        clickedBtn.className = "w-full py-3 px-6 bg-garden-100 border-2 border-garden-500 font-heading font-black text-lg text-garden-800 rounded-2xl transition-all";
        triggerConfetti(clickedBtn);
        window.speak("Bzzz! Amazing job! You got it right!");
      } else {
        playSound('incorrect');
        clickedBtn.className = "w-full py-3 px-6 bg-rose-100 border-2 border-rose-500 font-heading font-black text-lg text-rose-800 rounded-2xl transition-all";
        // Highlight the correct answer in green
        buttons.forEach(btn => {
          if (btn.textContent === q.answer) {
            btn.className = "w-full py-3 px-6 bg-garden-100 border-2 border-garden-500 font-heading font-black text-lg text-garden-800 rounded-2xl transition-all";
          }
        });
        window.speak("Nice try! Buzz says look at the green button for the right answer!");
      }

      // Proceed to next question after short delay
      setTimeout(() => {
        quizCurrentIndex++;
        if (quizCurrentIndex < TOTAL_QUIZ_QUESTIONS) {
          loadQuizQuestion();
        } else {
          showQuizResults();
        }
      }, 2500);
    }

    function showQuizResults() {
      document.getElementById("quizQuestionScreen").classList.add("hidden");
      document.getElementById("quizQuestionScreen").classList.remove("flex");
      document.getElementById("quizResultScreen").classList.remove("hidden");
      document.getElementById("quizResultScreen").classList.add("flex");

      playSound('success');

      const scorePct = (quizScore / TOTAL_QUIZ_QUESTIONS) * 100;
      const titleEl = document.getElementById("quizFinalTitle");
      const descEl = document.getElementById("quizFinalDescription");
      const scoreTextEl = document.getElementById("quizFinalScoreText");

      scoreTextEl.textContent = `${quizScore} / ${TOTAL_QUIZ_QUESTIONS}`;

      let voiceMessage = "";
      if (scorePct === 100) {
        titleEl.textContent = "🏆 Perfect Clock Master!";
        descEl.textContent = "Unbelievable! You answered every single question correctly! You are officially a Clock Master! Buzz is so proud of you!";
        voiceMessage = "Unbelievable! You got a perfect score! You are officially a clock master! Buzz is so proud of you!";
      } else if (scorePct >= 70) {
        titleEl.textContent = "🌟 Stellar Explorer!";
        descEl.textContent = `Wonderful job! You collected ${quizScore} stars! You are extremely good at reading clocks. Let's practice a bit more to get 20!`;
        voiceMessage = `Wonderful job! You collected ${quizScore} stars! You are extremely good at reading clocks. Let's practice a bit more next time!`;
      } else {
        titleEl.textContent = "⏰ Clock Apprentice!";
        descEl.textContent = `Great effort! You collected ${quizScore} stars. Let's read the learning secrets again and try one more time!`;
        voiceMessage = `Great effort! You collected ${quizScore} stars. Let's read the learning secrets again and try one more time to collect more stars!`;
      }

      window.speak(voiceMessage);
      triggerConfetti(document.getElementById("quizTrophyWrapper"));
    }

    window.restartQuiz = function() {
      startQuiz();
    };

    // Wow Detail: Confetti Burst
    function triggerConfetti(targetEl) {
      if (!targetEl) return;
      const rect = targetEl.getBoundingClientRect();
      const parent = document.body;
      const colors = ['#f59e0b', '#38bdf8', '#22c55e', '#f43f5e', '#a855f7'];

      for (let i = 0; i < 30; i++) {
        const confetti = document.createElement("div");
        confetti.className = "confetti";
        confetti.style.backgroundColor = colors[Math.floor(Math.random() * colors.length)];
        confetti.style.left = `${rect.left + window.scrollX + rect.width / 2 + (Math.random() - 0.5) * 60}px`;
        confetti.style.top = `${rect.top + window.scrollY + rect.height / 2 + (Math.random() - 0.5) * 40}px`;
        
        // Randomize direction and speed
        const angle = Math.random() * Math.PI * 2;
        const distance = Math.random() * 80 + 40;
        const destX = Math.cos(angle) * distance;
        const destY = Math.sin(angle) * distance;

        confetti.style.setProperty('--dest-x', `${destX}px`);
        confetti.style.setProperty('--dest-y', `${destY}px`);
        
        // Inject random fall speed
        confetti.style.animationDuration = `${0.6 + Math.random() * 0.8}s`;
        parent.appendChild(confetti);

        // Clean up
        setTimeout(() => {
          confetti.remove();
        }, 1500);
      }
    }

    // 6. REUSABLE LEGAL MODAL
    window.openLegal = function(title, bodyHtml) {
      playSound('click');
      document.getElementById("legalTitle").textContent = title;
      document.getElementById("legalBody").innerHTML = bodyHtml;
      document.getElementById("legalModal").style.display = "flex";
    };

    window.closeLegal = function() {
      playSound('click');
      document.getElementById("legalModal").style.display = "none";
    };

    // 7. INITIALIZATION ON LOAD
    generateMinuteTicks();
    updateClockHands(sandboxHour, sandboxMinute, "hourHand", "minuteHand");
    document.getElementById("currentYear").textContent = new Date().getFullYear();

    // Scroll reveal system
    const _io = new IntersectionObserver(entries => {
      entries.forEach(e => {
        if (e.isIntersecting) {
          e.target.classList.add('visible');
          _io.unobserve(e.target);
        }
      });
    }, { threshold: 0.08, rootMargin: '0px 0px -40px 0px' });
    
    document.querySelectorAll('.reveal').forEach(el => _io.observe(el));
    
    // Safety Net: Force reveal after 1 second if observer fails
    setTimeout(function() {
      document.querySelectorAll('.reveal').forEach(function(el) {
        el.style.opacity = '1';
        el.style.transform = 'none';
      });
    }, 1000);

    // Smooth scroll for anchors
    document.querySelectorAll('a[href^="#"]').forEach(a => {
      a.addEventListener('click', function(e) {
        e.preventDefault();
        const targetId = this.getAttribute('href');
        if (targetId === '#') return;
        const el = document.querySelector(targetId);
        if (el) {
          window.scrollTo({
            top: el.getBoundingClientRect().top + window.pageYOffset - 90,
            behavior: 'smooth'
          });
        }
      });
    });

  })();
  </script>

    lucide.createIcons();
  </script>

</body>
</html>

  <!-- CORE JAVASCRIPT BLOCK (IIFE WRAPPED FOR SAFETY & TO PREVENT REDECLARATION CRASHES) -->
  <script>
  (function() {
    // 1. STATE VARIABLES
    let voiceEnabled = true;
    let sandboxHour = 12;
    let sandboxMinute = 0;
    let sandboxPeriod = "PM";

    // Days of the Week State
    let selectedDayIndex = 1; // Default to Monday
    let dayQuizCurrentIndex = 0;
    let dayQuizScore = 0;
    const TOTAL_DAY_QUESTIONS = 10;
    const daysOfWeek = ["Sunday", "Monday", "Tuesday", "Wednesday", "Thursday", "Friday", "Saturday"];
    
    const dayQuizQuestions = [
      {
        target: "Monday",
        relation: "after",
        questionText: "What day comes AFTER Sunday, Charistine?",
        options: ["Monday", "Tuesday", "Saturday", "Friday"],
        answer: "Monday"
      },
      {
        target: "Tuesday",
        relation: "before",
        questionText: "What day comes BEFORE Tuesday, Charistine?",
        options: ["Sunday", "Monday", "Wednesday", "Thursday"],
        answer: "Monday"
      },
      {
        target: "Monday",
        relation: "after",
        questionText: "What day comes AFTER Monday, Charistine?",
        options: ["Wednesday", "Tuesday", "Sunday", "Friday"],
        answer: "Tuesday"
      },
      {
        target: "Monday",
        relation: "before",
        questionText: "What day comes BEFORE Monday, Charistine?",
        options: ["Saturday", "Sunday", "Tuesday", "Wednesday"],
        answer: "Sunday"
      },
      {
        target: "Wednesday",
        relation: "after",
        questionText: "What day comes AFTER Wednesday, Charistine?",
        options: ["Thursday", "Tuesday", "Friday", "Monday"],
        answer: "Thursday"
      },
      {
        target: "Friday",
        relation: "before",
        questionText: "What day comes BEFORE Friday, Charistine?",
        options: ["Wednesday", "Thursday", "Saturday", "Monday"],
        answer: "Thursday"
      },
      {
        target: "Friday",
        relation: "after",
        questionText: "What day comes AFTER Friday, Charistine?",
        options: ["Thursday", "Saturday", "Sunday", "Monday"],
        answer: "Saturday"
      },
      {
        target: "Sunday",
        relation: "before",
        questionText: "What day comes BEFORE Sunday, Charistine?",
        options: ["Saturday", "Monday", "Friday", "Wednesday"],
        answer: "Saturday"
      },
      {
        target: "Tuesday",
        relation: "after",
        questionText: "What day comes AFTER Tuesday, Charistine?",
        options: ["Monday", "Wednesday", "Thursday", "Sunday"],
        answer: "Wednesday"
      },
      {
        target: "Thursday",
        relation: "before",
        questionText: "What day comes BEFORE Thursday, Charistine?",
        options: ["Tuesday", "Wednesday", "Friday", "Monday"],
        answer: "Wednesday"
      }
    ];

    // Quiz State
    let quizCurrentIndex = 0;
    let quizScore = 0;
    const TOTAL_QUIZ_QUESTIONS = 20;

    // AUDIO SYNTHESIS SYSTEM (Web Audio API)
    let audioCtx = null;
    function initAudioContext() {
      if (!audioCtx) {
        audioCtx = new (window.AudioContext || window.webkitAudioContext)();
      }
    }

    function playSound(type) {
      try {
        initAudioContext();
        if (audioCtx.state === 'suspended') {
          audioCtx.resume();
        }
        
        const osc = audioCtx.createOscillator();
        const gainNode = audioCtx.createGain();
        osc.connect(gainNode);
        gainNode.connect(audioCtx.destination);

        const now = audioCtx.currentTime;

        if (type === 'correct') {
          osc.type = 'triangle';
          osc.frequency.setValueAtTime(523.25, now); // C5
          osc.frequency.setValueAtTime(659.25, now + 0.1); // E5
          osc.frequency.setValueAtTime(783.99, now + 0.2); // G5
          osc.frequency.setValueAtTime(1046.50, now + 0.3); // C6
          gainNode.gain.setValueAtTime(0.15, now);
          gainNode.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.01, now + 0.5);
          osc.start(now);
          osc.stop(now + 0.5);
        } else if (type === 'incorrect') {
          osc.type = 'sawtooth';
          osc.frequency.setValueAtTime(150, now);
          osc.frequency.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(80, now + 0.3);
          gainNode.gain.setValueAtTime(0.12, now);
          gainNode.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.01, now + 0.3);
          osc.start(now);
          osc.stop(now + 0.3);
        } else if (type === 'click') {
          osc.type = 'sine';
          osc.frequency.setValueAtTime(400, now);
          osc.frequency.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(100, now + 0.08);
          gainNode.gain.setValueAtTime(0.2, now);
          gainNode.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.01, now + 0.08);
          osc.start(now);
          osc.stop(now + 0.08);
        } else if (type === 'success') {
          osc.type = 'sine';
          osc.frequency.setValueAtTime(261.63, now); // C4
          osc.frequency.setValueAtTime(329.63, now + 0.15); // E4
          osc.frequency.setValueAtTime(392.00, now + 0.3); // G4
          osc.frequency.setValueAtTime(523.25, now + 0.45); // C5
          gainNode.gain.setValueAtTime(0.2, now);
          gainNode.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.01, now + 0.8);
          osc.start(now);
          osc.stop(now + 0.8);
        }
      } catch (err) {
        console.warn("Web Audio API blocked or unsupported:", err);
      }
    }

    // 2. SPEECH SYNTHESIS ASSISTANT
    window.speak = function(text) {
      if (!voiceEnabled) return;
      try {
        window.speechSynthesis.cancel(); // Stop current speaking
        const utterance = new SpeechSynthesisUtterance(text);
        utterance.rate = 0.85; // Slower, clear rate for child comprehension
        utterance.pitch = 1.25; // Warm, friendly, higher pitch tone
        window.speechSynthesis.speak(utterance);
      } catch (err) {
        console.error("Speech Synthesis failed:", err);
      }
    };

    window.toggleVoice = function() {
      voiceEnabled = !voiceEnabled;
      const btn = document.getElementById("voiceToggleBtn");
      const icon = document.getElementById("voiceIcon");
      const statusText = document.getElementById("voiceStatusText");
      
      if (voiceEnabled) {
        btn.className = "flex items-center gap-2 px-3 sm:px-4 py-2 bg-honey-100 hover:bg-honey-200 border-2 border-honey-500 rounded-full text-honey-800 font-bold text-xs sm:text-sm transition-all transform hover:scale-105";
        icon.setAttribute("data-lucide", "volume-2");
        statusText.textContent = "Voice: ON";
        window.speak("Voice is now on! Let's learn together, Charistine!");
      } else {
        btn.className = "flex items-center gap-2 px-3 sm:px-4 py-2 bg-slate-100 hover:bg-slate-200 border-2 border-slate-400 rounded-full text-slate-600 font-bold text-xs sm:text-sm transition-all transform hover:scale-105";
        icon.setAttribute("data-lucide", "volume-x");
        statusText.textContent = "Voice: OFF";
        window.speechSynthesis.cancel();
      }
      lucide.createIcons();
      playSound('click');
    };

    window.speakWelcome = function() {
      window.speak("Bzzz! Hello there, Charistine! Welcome to my Clock and Day Academy. Let's learn how to read watches and understand before and after for the days of the week. Let's play!");
    };

    // Lesson specific speak text
    window.speakLesson = function(lessonKey) {
      let text = "";
      if (lessonKey === 'anatomy') {
        text = "Secret number one! Meet the clock face. The clock is like a round neighborhood with 12 houses on it. The numbers go in a big circle from 1 all the way to 12. The top house is always 12, and the bottom house is always 6!";
      } else if (lessonKey === 'hours') {
        text = "Secret number two! The short hour hand. The clock has two main pointers called hands. The short, thick blue hand is the Hour Hand. Whichever number it points to tells us the hour! If it points right at 3, it is 3 o'clock!";
      } else if (lessonKey === 'minutes') {
        text = "Secret number three! The long minute hand. The long, tall red hand is the Minute Hand. It measures the tiny, fast minutes! When it points straight up at 12, it means zero-zero minutes, or o'clock. When it points straight down at 6, it means 30 minutes, or half-past!";
      } else if (lessonKey === 'secrets') {
        text = "Secret number four! Magic time numbers! Time has a magical pattern. Sixty seconds makes one minute. Sixty minutes makes one whole hour. And twenty-four hours makes one full day of fun!";
      }
      window.speak(text);
    };

    // 3. DAYS OF THE WEEK INTERACTIVE SYSTEM
    window.selectDayFinder = function(index) {
      selectedDayIndex = index;
      playSound('click');
      
      // Update visual active state on train buttons
      for (let i = 0; i < 7; i++) {
        const btn = document.getElementById(`trainBtn-${i}`);
        if (btn) {
          if (i === index) {
            btn.classList.add('ring-4', 'ring-violet-500', 'scale-110', 'z-10');
          } else {
            btn.classList.remove('ring-4', 'ring-violet-500', 'scale-110', 'z-10');
          }
        }
      }

      const currentDay = daysOfWeek[index];
      const beforeDay = daysOfWeek[(index - 1 + 7) % 7];
      const afterDay = daysOfWeek[(index + 1) % 7];

      // Update text nodes
      document.getElementById("finderCurrentDay").textContent = currentDay;
      document.getElementById("finderBeforeDay").textContent = beforeDay;
      document.getElementById("finderAfterDay").textContent = afterDay;

      // Speak it
      speakFinderState();
    };

    window.speakFinderState = function() {
      const currentDay = daysOfWeek[selectedDayIndex];
      const beforeDay = daysOfWeek[(selectedDayIndex - 1 + 7) % 7];
      const afterDay = daysOfWeek[(selectedDayIndex + 1) % 7];
      const message = `${currentDay}! The day BEFORE ${currentDay} is ${beforeDay}. The day AFTER ${currentDay} is ${afterDay}!`;
      window.speak(message);
    };

    // DAYS OF THE WEEK QUIZ ENGINE
    window.startDayQuiz = function() {
      playSound('click');
      dayQuizCurrentIndex = 0;
      dayQuizScore = 0;
      
      document.getElementById("dayQuizStartScreen").classList.add("hidden");
      document.getElementById("dayQuizResultScreen").classList.add("hidden");
      document.getElementById("dayQuizQuestionScreen").classList.remove("hidden");
      document.getElementById("dayQuizQuestionScreen").classList.add("flex");

      buildDayQuizProgressDots();
      loadDayQuizQuestion();
    };

    function buildDayQuizProgressDots() {
      const container = document.getElementById("dayQuizDotsContainer");
      if (!container) return;
      container.innerHTML = "";
      for (let i = 0; i < TOTAL_DAY_QUESTIONS; i++) {
        const dot = document.createElement("div");
        dot.className = "w-2.5 h-2.5 rounded-full bg-violet-300 transition-all";
        dot.id = `dayQuizDot-${i}`;
        container.appendChild(dot);
      }
    }

    function loadDayQuizQuestion() {
      const q = dayQuizQuestions[dayQuizCurrentIndex];
      
      document.getElementById("dayQuizCurrentNum").textContent = dayQuizCurrentIndex + 1;
      document.getElementById("dayQuizScoreText").textContent = `Stars: ${dayQuizScore}`;

      // Highlight active dot
      for (let i = 0; i < TOTAL_DAY_QUESTIONS; i++) {
        const dot = document.getElementById(`dayQuizDot-${i}`);
        if (dot) {
          if (i === dayQuizCurrentIndex) {
            dot.className = "w-3.5 h-3.5 rounded-full bg-violet-600 ring-2 ring-white scale-125 transition-all";
          } else if (i < dayQuizCurrentIndex) {
            dot.className = "w-2.5 h-2.5 rounded-full bg-garden-500 transition-all";
          } else {
            dot.className = "w-2.5 h-2.5 rounded-full bg-violet-300 transition-all";
          }
        }
      }

      // Question Text
      document.getElementById("dayQuizQuestionTitle").textContent = q.questionText;

      // Visual demonstration of before/after
      const visualContainer = document.getElementById("dayQuizVisualContainer");
      visualContainer.innerHTML = "";
      
      if (q.relation === "after") {
        visualContainer.innerHTML = `
          <div class="flex items-center gap-2 sm:gap-4 font-heading font-black text-sm sm:text-base">
            <div class="px-4 py-2 bg-slate-100 text-slate-700 rounded-xl border-2 border-slate-300">${q.target}</div>
            <div class="text-violet-500 animate-bounce-gentle"><i data-lucide="arrow-right" class="w-6 h-6"></i></div>
            <div class="px-4 py-2 bg-violet-100 text-violet-700 rounded-xl border-4 border-dashed border-violet-400 animate-pulse-soft">? What's Next ?</div>
          </div>
        `;
      } else {
        visualContainer.innerHTML = `
          <div class="flex items-center gap-2 sm:gap-4 font-heading font-black text-sm sm:text-base">
            <div class="px-4 py-2 bg-violet-100 text-violet-700 rounded-xl border-4 border-dashed border-violet-400 animate-pulse-soft">? What's Before ?</div>
            <div class="text-violet-500 animate-bounce-gentle"><i data-lucide="arrow-right" class="w-6 h-6"></i></div>
            <div class="px-4 py-2 bg-slate-100 text-slate-700 rounded-xl border-2 border-slate-300">${q.target}</div>
          </div>
        `;
      }
      lucide.createIcons();

      // Options
      const container = document.getElementById("dayQuizAnswersContainer");
      container.innerHTML = "";
      q.options.forEach(opt => {
        const btn = document.createElement("button");
        btn.className = "w-full py-3 px-6 bg-slate-50 hover:bg-violet-100 border-2 border-slate-300 hover:border-violet-400 font-heading font-black text-lg text-slate-700 rounded-2xl transition-all transform active:scale-95";
        btn.textContent = opt;
        btn.onclick = () => handleDayAnswerSubmit(opt, btn);
        container.appendChild(btn);
      });

      setTimeout(() => {
        window.speak(q.questionText);
      }, 300);
    }

    window.speakDayQuizQuestion = function() {
      const q = dayQuizQuestions[dayQuizCurrentIndex];
      window.speak(q.questionText);
    };

    function handleDayAnswerSubmit(selectedOption, clickedBtn) {
      const q = dayQuizQuestions[dayQuizCurrentIndex];
      const isCorrect = selectedOption === q.answer;

      const container = document.getElementById("dayQuizAnswersContainer");
      const buttons = container.querySelectorAll("button");
      buttons.forEach(btn => btn.disabled = true);

      if (isCorrect) {
        dayQuizScore++;
        playSound('correct');
        clickedBtn.className = "w-full py-3 px-6 bg-garden-100 border-2 border-garden-500 font-heading font-black text-lg text-garden-800 rounded-2xl transition-all";
        triggerConfetti(clickedBtn);
        window.speak(`Bzzz! That is correct, Charistine! ${selectedOption} is right!`);
      } else {
        playSound('incorrect');
        clickedBtn.className = "w-full py-3 px-6 bg-rose-100 border-2 border-rose-500 font-heading font-black text-lg text-rose-800 rounded-2xl transition-all";
        buttons.forEach(btn => {
          if (btn.textContent === q.answer) {
            btn.className = "w-full py-3 px-6 bg-garden-100 border-2 border-garden-500 font-heading font-black text-lg text-garden-800 rounded-2xl transition-all";
          }
        });
        window.speak(`Nice try, Charistine! The correct answer is ${q.answer}!`);
      }

      setTimeout(() => {
        dayQuizCurrentIndex++;
        if (dayQuizCurrentIndex < TOTAL_DAY_QUESTIONS) {
          loadDayQuizQuestion();
        } else {
          showDayQuizResults();
        }
      }, 2500);
    }

    function showDayQuizResults() {
      document.getElementById("dayQuizQuestionScreen").classList.add("hidden");
      document.getElementById("dayQuizQuestionScreen").classList.remove("flex");
      document.getElementById("dayQuizResultScreen").classList.remove("hidden");
      document.getElementById("dayQuizResultScreen").classList.add("flex");

      playSound('success');

      const scorePct = (dayQuizScore / TOTAL_DAY_QUESTIONS) * 100;
      const titleEl = document.getElementById("dayQuizFinalTitle");
      const descEl = document.getElementById("dayQuizFinalDescription");
      const scoreTextEl = document.getElementById("dayQuizFinalScoreText");

      scoreTextEl.textContent = `${dayQuizScore} / ${TOTAL_DAY_QUESTIONS}`;

      let voiceMessage = "";
      if (scorePct === 100) {
        titleEl.textContent = "🏆 Day Champion, Charistine!";
        descEl.textContent = "Oh my goodness, Charistine! You got a PERFECT score! You know all the days of the week, and exactly what comes before and after! Buzz is doing a happy bee dance!";
        voiceMessage = "Oh my goodness, Charistine! You got a perfect score! You know all the days of the week, and exactly what comes before and after! Buzz is doing a happy bee dance!";
      } else if (scorePct >= 70) {
        titleEl.textContent = "🌟 Day Explorer, Charistine!";
        descEl.textContent = `Spectacular job, Charistine! You collected ${dayQuizScore} stars! You are super close to a perfect score. Let's try again to get all 10 stars!`;
        voiceMessage = `Spectacular job, Charistine! You collected ${dayQuizScore} stars! You are super close to a perfect score. Let's try again to get all 10 stars!`;
      } else {
        titleEl.textContent = "📅 Keep Learning, Charistine!";
        descEl.textContent = `Good try, Charistine! You got ${dayQuizScore} stars. Let's practice with Buzz's Day Train above and try again!`;
        voiceMessage = `Good try, Charistine! You got ${dayQuizScore} stars. Let's practice with Buzz's Day Train above and try again!`;
      }

      window.speak(voiceMessage);
      triggerConfetti(document.getElementById("dayQuizTrophyWrapper"));
    }

    window.restartDayQuiz = function() {
      startDayQuiz();
    };

    // 4. INTERACTIVE TAB SYSTEM (classList based)
    window.showTab = function(id, btn) {
      playSound('click');
      document.querySelectorAll('.tab-panel').forEach(p => p.classList.remove('active'));
      document.querySelectorAll('.tab-btn').forEach(b => {
        b.className = "tab-btn px-6 py-3 bg-slate-100 border-2 border-transparent text-slate-600 font-heading font-black text-base rounded-2xl flex items-center gap-2 transition-all";
      });
      
      const panel = document.getElementById(id);
      if (panel) panel.classList.add('active');
      if (btn) {
        btn.className = "tab-btn active px-6 py-3 bg-honey-100 border-2 border-honey-400 text-slate-800 font-heading font-black text-base rounded-2xl flex items-center gap-2 transition-all";
      }
    };

    // 5. ANALOG CLOCK MECHANICS & GENERATOR
    function generateMinuteTicks() {
      const group = document.getElementById("minuteTicks");
      if (!group) return;
      group.innerHTML = "";
      for (let i = 0; i < 60; i++) {
        if (i % 5 === 0) continue; // Skip numbers
        const angle = (i * 6) * Math.PI / 180;
        const x1 = 150 + 115 * Math.sin(angle);
        const y1 = 150 - 115 * Math.cos(angle);
        const x2 = 150 + 120 * Math.sin(angle);
        const y2 = 150 - 120

                    <line id="quizHourHand" x1="150" y1="150" x2="150" y2="95" stroke="#0ea5e9" stroke-width="10" stroke-linecap="round" />
                    <line id="quizMinuteHand" x1="150" y1="150" x2="150" y2="70" stroke="#f43f5e" stroke-width="6" stroke-linecap="round" />

                  </svg>

            <!-- Audio Helper for Quiz -->
            <button onclick="speakQuizQuestion()" class="px-5 py-2.5 bg-sky-100 hover:bg-sky-200 text-sky-800 font-bold rounded-xl flex items-center gap-2 transition-all">
              <i data-lucide="volume-2" class="w-5 h-5"></i> Speak Question Aloud!

              <!-- Trophy SVG -->

                <path d="M18 2H6a2 2 0 00-2 2v3c0 2.21 1.79 4 4 4h1.09c.47 1.81 1.91 3.24 3.76 3.75V19H9a1 1 0 000 2h6a1 1 0 000-2h-3.85v-4.25c1.85-.51 3.29-1.94 3.76-3.75H16c2.21 0 4-1.79 4-4V4a2 2 0 00-2-2zm-10 7c-1.1 0-2-.9-2-2V4h2v3c0 1.1-.9 2-2 2zm10-2c0 1.1-.9 2-2 2s-2-.9-2-2V4h4v3z"/>
              </svg>
            </div>
            <h3 class="font-heading font-black text-3xl text-slate-800 mb-2" id="quizFinalTitle">Spectacular Job!</h3>
            <p class="text-slate-600 max-w-md mb-6" id="quizFinalDescription">
              You collected 18 stars! You are officially an honorary Clock Explorer! Keep practicing to get all 20!
            </p>
            
            <div class="bg-slate-50 p-6 rounded-2xl border-2 border-slate-200 w-full max-w-sm mb-8">
              <span class="block text-sm font-bold text-slate-500 uppercase">Your Mastery Score</span>
              <span class="block font-heading font-black text-5xl text-honey-500 mt-1" id="quizFinalScoreText">18 / 20</span>

            <button onclick="restartQuiz()" class="px-10 py-4 bg-garden-500 hover:bg-garden-600 border-b-4 border-garden-700 text-white font-heading font-black text-xl rounded-2xl shadow-md transition-all transform hover:-translate-y-1">
              Play Again! 🔄
            </button>

      <!-- Links (Modal Triggered - No Dead Links) -->

  <!-- CORE JAVASCRIPT BLOCK (IIFE WRAPPED FOR SAFETY & TO PREVENT REDECLARATION CRASHES) -->
  <script>
  (function() {
    // 1. STATE VARIABLES
    let voiceEnabled = true;
    let sandboxHour = 12;
    let sandboxMinute = 0;
    let sandboxPeriod = "PM";

    // Days of the Week State
    const daysArray = ["Sunday", "Monday", "Tuesday", "Wednesday", "Thursday", "Friday", "Saturday"];
    let selectedDayIdx = 2; // Default to Tuesday
    let dayQuizCurrentIndex = 0;
    let dayQuizScore = 0;
    const TOTAL_DAY_QUESTIONS = 5;

    const dayQuizQuestions = [
      { q: "What day comes AFTER Sunday? ☀️", before: false, reference: "Sunday", answer: "Monday", options: ["Saturday", "Monday", "Tuesday"] },
      { q: "What day comes BEFORE Wednesday? 🗓️", before: true, reference: "Wednesday", answer: "Tuesday", options: ["Tuesday", "Thursday", "Monday"] },
      { q: "What day comes AFTER Friday? 🎉", before: false, reference: "Friday", answer: "Saturday", options: ["Thursday", "Saturday", "Sunday"] },
      { q: "What day comes BEFORE Monday? 🚀", before: true, reference: "Monday", answer: "Sunday", options: ["Sunday", "Tuesday", "Friday"] },
      { q: "What day comes AFTER Thursday? 🌳", before: false, reference: "Thursday", answer: "Friday", options: ["Wednesday", "Friday", "Saturday"] }
    ];

    // Quiz State
    let quizCurrentIndex = 0;
    let quizScore = 0;
    const TOTAL_QUIZ_QUESTIONS = 20;

    // AUDIO SYNTHESIS SYSTEM (Web Audio API)
    let audioCtx = null;
    function initAudioContext() {
      if (!audioCtx) {
        audioCtx = new (window.AudioContext || window.webkitAudioContext)();
      }
    }

    function playSound(type) {
      try {
        initAudioContext();
        if (audioCtx.state === 'suspended') {
          audioCtx.resume();
        }
        
        const osc = audioCtx.createOscillator();
        const gainNode = audioCtx.createGain();
        osc.connect(gainNode);
        gainNode.connect(audioCtx.destination);

        const now = audioCtx.currentTime;

        if (type === 'correct') {
          osc.type = 'triangle';
          osc.frequency.setValueAtTime(523.25, now); // C5
          osc.frequency.setValueAtTime(659.25, now + 0.1); // E5
          osc.frequency.setValueAtTime(783.99, now + 0.2); // G5
          osc.frequency.setValueAtTime(1046.50, now + 0.3); // C6
          gainNode.gain.setValueAtTime(0.15, now);
          gainNode.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.01, now + 0.5);
          osc.start(now);
          osc.stop(now + 0.5);
        } else if (type === 'incorrect') {
          osc.type = 'sawtooth';
          osc.frequency.setValueAtTime(150, now);
          osc.frequency.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(80, now + 0.3);
          gainNode.gain.setValueAtTime(0.12, now);
          gainNode.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.01, now + 0.3);
          osc.start(now);
          osc.stop(now + 0.3);
        } else if (type === 'click') {
          osc.type = 'sine';
          osc.frequency.setValueAtTime(400, now);
          osc.frequency.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(100, now + 0.08);
          gainNode.gain.setValueAtTime(0.2, now);
          gainNode.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.01, now + 0.08);
          osc.start(now);
          osc.stop(now + 0.08);
        } else if (type === 'success') {
          osc.type = 'sine';
          osc.frequency.setValueAtTime(261.63, now); // C4
          osc.frequency.setValueAtTime(329.63, now + 0.15); // E4
          osc.frequency.setValueAtTime(392.00, now + 0.3); // G4
          osc.frequency.setValueAtTime(523.25, now + 0.45); // C5
          gainNode.gain.setValueAtTime(0.2, now);
          gainNode.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.01, now + 0.8);
          osc.start(now);
          osc.stop(now + 0.8);
        }
      } catch (err) {
        console.warn("Web Audio API blocked or unsupported:", err);
      }
    }

    // 2. SPEECH SYNTHESIS ASSISTANT
    window.speak = function(text) {
      if (!voiceEnabled) return;
      try {
        window.speechSynthesis.cancel(); // Stop current speaking
        const utterance = new SpeechSynthesisUtterance(text);
        utterance.rate = 0.85; // Slower, clear rate for child comprehension
        utterance.pitch = 1.25; // Warm, friendly, higher pitch tone
        window.speechSynthesis.speak(utterance);
      } catch (err) {
        console.error("Speech Synthesis failed:", err);
      }
    };

    window.toggleVoice = function() {
      voiceEnabled = !voiceEnabled;
      const btn = document.getElementById("voiceToggleBtn");
      const icon = document.getElementById("voiceIcon");
      const statusText = document.getElementById("voiceStatusText");
      
      if (voiceEnabled) {
        btn.className = "flex items-center gap-2 px-4 py-2 bg-honey-100 hover:bg-honey-200 border-2 border-honey-500 rounded-full text-honey-800 font-bold text-sm transition-all transform hover:scale-105";
        icon.setAttribute("data-lucide", "volume-2");
        statusText.textContent = "Voice: ON";
        window.speak("Voice is now on! Let's tell time together, Christine!");
      } else {
        btn.className = "flex items-center gap-2 px-4 py-2 bg-slate-100 hover:bg-slate-200 border-2 border-slate-400 rounded-full text-slate-600 font-bold text-sm transition-all transform hover:scale-105";
        icon.setAttribute("data-lucide", "volume-x");
        statusText.textContent = "Voice: OFF";
        window.speechSynthesis.cancel();
      }
      lucide.createIcons();
      playSound('click');
    };

    window.speakWelcome = function() {
      window.speak("Bzzz! Hello there, Christine! Welcome to my Clock and Calendar Academy. Let's learn how to read watches and learn the days of the week! Click the colorful buttons to begin the adventure!");
    };

    // Lesson specific speak text
    window.speakLesson = function(lessonKey) {
      let text = "";
      if (lessonKey === 'anatomy') {
        text = "Secret number one! Meet the clock face. The clock is like a round neighborhood with 12 houses on it. The numbers go in a big circle from 1 all the way to 12. The top house is always 12, and the bottom house is always 6!";
      } else if (lessonKey === 'hours') {
        text = "Secret number two! The short hour hand. The clock has two main pointers called hands. The short, thick blue hand is the Hour Hand. Whichever number it points to tells us the hour! If it points right at 3, it is 3 o'clock!";
      } else if (lessonKey === 'minutes') {
        text = "Secret number three! The long minute hand. The long, tall red hand is the Minute Hand. It measures the tiny, fast minutes! When it points straight up at 12, it means zero-zero minutes, or o'clock. When it points straight down at 6, it means 30 minutes, or half-past!";
      } else if (lessonKey === 'secrets') {
        text = "Secret number four! Magic time numbers! Time has a magical pattern. Sixty seconds makes one minute. Sixty minutes makes one whole hour. And twenty-four hours makes one full day of fun!";
      }
      window.speak(text);
    };

    // 3. INTERACTIVE TAB SYSTEM (classList based)
    window.showTab = function(id, btn) {
      playSound('click');
      document.querySelectorAll('.tab-panel').forEach(p => p.classList.remove('active'));
      document.querySelectorAll('.tab-btn').forEach(b => {
        b.className = "tab-btn px-6 py-3 bg-slate-100 border-2 border-transparent text-slate-600 font-heading font-black text-base rounded-2xl flex items-center gap-2 transition-all";
      });
      
      const panel = document.getElementById(id);
      if (panel) panel.classList.add('active');
      if (btn) {
        btn.className = "tab-btn active px-6 py-3 bg-honey-100 border-2 border-honey-400 text-slate-800 font-heading font-black text-base rounded-2xl flex items-center gap-2 transition-all";
      }
    };

    // 4. ANALOG CLOCK MECHANICS & GENERATOR
    function generateMinuteTicks() {
      const group = document.getElementById("minuteTicks");
      if (!group) return;
      group.innerHTML = "";
      for (let i = 0; i < 60; i++) {
        if (i % 5 === 0) continue; // Skip numbers
        const angle = (i * 6) * Math.PI / 180;
        const x1 = 150 + 115 * Math.sin(angle);
        const y1 = 150 - 115 * Math.cos(angle);
        const x2 = 150 + 120 * Math.sin(angle);
        const y2 = 150 - 120 * Math.cos(angle);
        
        const line = document.createElementNS("http://www.w3.org/2000/svg", "line");
        line.setAttribute("x1", x1);
        line.setAttribute("y1", y1);
        line.setAttribute("x2", x2);
        line.setAttribute("y2", y2);
        group.appendChild(line);
      }
    }

    function updateClockHands(hour, minute, hourHandId, minuteHandId) {
      const hHand = document.getElementById(hourHandId);
      const mHand = document.getElementById(minuteHandId);
      if (!hHand || !mHand) return;

      const mAngle = minute * 6;
      const hAngle = (hour % 12) * 30 + minute * 0.5;

      hHand.setAttribute("transform", `rotate(${hAngle})`);
      mHand.setAttribute("transform", `rotate(${mAngle})`);
    }

    // Adjust Sandbox Time
    window.adjustTime = function(hDelta, mDelta) {
      playSound('click');
      sandboxHour += hDelta;
      sandboxMinute += mDelta;

      if (sandboxMinute >= 60) {
        sandboxMinute = 0;
        sandboxHour += 1;
      } else if (sandboxMinute < 0) {
        sandboxMinute = 55;
        sandboxHour -= 1;
      }

      if (sandboxHour > 12) {
        sandboxHour = 1;
        sandboxPeriod = (sandboxPeriod === "AM") ? "PM" : "AM";
      } else if (sandboxHour < 1) {
        sandboxHour = 12;
        sandboxPeriod = (sandboxPeriod === "AM") ? "PM" : "AM";
      }

      document.getElementById("digitalHour").textContent = sandboxHour;
      document.getElementById("digitalMinute").textContent = String(sandboxMinute).padStart(2, '0');
      document.getElementById("digitalPeriod").textContent = sandboxPeriod;

      updateClockHands(sandboxHour, sandboxMinute, "hourHand", "minuteHand");
      updateDayNightTheme(sandboxHour, sandboxPeriod);
    };

    function updateDayNightTheme(hour, period) {
      const bg = document.getElementById("clockBackground");
      const indicator = document.getElementById("skyIndicator");
      if (!bg || !indicator) return;

      const isNight = (hour >= 7 && hour < 12 && period === "PM") || (hour === 12 && period === "AM") || (hour < 6 && period === "AM");
      
      if (isNight) {
        bg.style.background = "linear-gradient(135deg, #1e1b4b 0%, #312e81 100%)";
        indicator.textContent = "🌙";
      } else {
        bg.style.background = "linear-gradient(135deg, #fef08a 0%, #fef3c7 100%)";
        indicator.textContent = "☀️";
      }
    }

    window.speakCurrentSandboxTime = function() {
      let minText = "";
      if (sandboxMinute === 0) {
        minText = "o'clock";
      } else if (sandboxMinute === 30) {
        minText = "thirty";
      } else {
        minText = sandboxMinute;
      }
      window.speak(`The sandbox clock is set to ${sandboxHour} ${minText} ${sandboxPeriod}!`);
    };

    // 4b. DAYS OF THE WEEK INTERACTIVE SYSTEM
    window.selectDay = function(idx) {
      playSound('click');
      selectedDayIdx = idx;
      
      // Update button highlights
      for (let i = 0; i < 7; i++) {
        const btn = document.getElementById(`day-btn-${i}`);
        if (btn) {
          if (i === idx) {
            btn.classList.add("ring-4", "ring-rose-400", "scale-105");
          } else {
            btn.classList.remove("ring-4", "ring-rose-400", "scale-105");
          }
        }
      }

      const today = daysArray[idx];
      const before = daysArray[(idx + 6) % 7];
      const after = daysArray[(idx + 1) % 7];

      document.getElementById("day-today-text").textContent = today;
      document.getElementById("day-before-text").textContent = before;
      document.getElementById("day-after-text").textContent = after;

      window.speakDayConcept();
    };

    window.speakDayConcept = function() {
      const today = daysArray[selectedDayIdx];
      const before = daysArray[(selectedDayIdx + 6) % 7];
      const after = daysArray[(selectedDayIdx + 1) % 7];
      window.speak(`Christine, if today is ${today}, the day before is ${before}, and the day after is ${after}!`);
    };

    // 4c. CHRISTINE'S DAY QUIZ ENGINE
    window.startDayQuiz = function() {
      playSound('click');
      dayQuizCurrentIndex = 0;
      dayQuizScore = 0;

      document.getElementById("dayQuizStartScreen").classList.add("hidden");
      document.getElementById("dayQuizResultScreen").classList.add("hidden");
      document.getElementById("dayQuizQuestionScreen").classList.remove("hidden");
      document.getElementById("dayQuizQuestionScreen").classList.add("flex");

      buildDayQuizProgressDots();
      loadDayQuizQuestion();
    };

    function buildDayQuizProgressDots() {
      const container = document.getElementById("dayQuizDotsContainer");
      if (!container) return;
      container.innerHTML = "";
      for (let i = 0; i < TOTAL_DAY_QUESTIONS; i++) {
        const dot = document.createElement("div");
        dot.className = "w-2.5 h-2.5 rounded-full bg-emerald-300 transition-all";
        dot.id = `dayQuizDot-${i}`;
        container.appendChild(dot);
      }
    }

    function loadDayQuizQuestion() {
      const q = dayQuizQuestions[dayQuizCurrentIndex];

      document.getElementById("dayQuizCurrentNum").textContent = dayQuizCurrentIndex + 1;
      document.getElementById("dayQuizScoreText").textContent = `Stars: ${dayQuizScore}`;

      // Highlight active dot
      for (let i = 0; i < TOTAL_DAY_QUESTIONS; i++) {
        const dot = document.getElementById(`dayQuizDot-${i}`);
        if (dot) {
          if (i === dayQuizCurrentIndex) {
            dot.className = "w-3.5 h-3.5 rounded-full bg-emerald-600 ring-2 ring-white scale-125 transition-all";
          } else if (i < dayQuizCurrentIndex) {
            dot.className = "w-2.5 h-2.5 rounded-full bg-garden-500 transition-all";
          } else {
            dot.className = "w-2.5 h-2.5 rounded-full bg-emerald-300 transition-all";
          }
        }
      }

      document.getElementById("dayQuizQuestionTitle").textContent = q.q;

      const container = document.getElementById("dayQuizAnswersContainer");
      container.innerHTML = "";
      q.options.forEach(opt => {
        const btn = document.createElement("button");
        btn.className = "w-full py-4 px-6 bg-slate-100 hover:bg-emerald-100 border-2 border-slate-300 hover:border-emerald-400 font-heading font-black text-xl text-slate-700 rounded-2xl transition-all transform active:scale-95";
        btn.textContent = opt;
        btn.onclick = () => handleDayQuizAnswer(opt, btn);
        container.appendChild(btn);
      });

      setTimeout(() => {
        window.speakDayQuizQuestion();
      }, 300);
    }

    window.speakDayQuizQuestion = function() {
      const q = dayQuizQuestions[dayQuizCurrentIndex];
      window.speak(`Christine, ${q.q}`);
    };

    function handleDayQuizAnswer(selected, clickedBtn) {
      const q = dayQuizQuestions[dayQuizCurrentIndex];
      const isCorrect = selected === q.answer;

      const container = document.getElementById("dayQuizAnswersContainer");
      const buttons = container.querySelectorAll("button");
      buttons.forEach(btn => btn.disabled = true);

      if (isCorrect) {
        dayQuizScore++;
        playSound('correct');
        clickedBtn.className = "w-full py-4 px-6 bg-garden-100 border-2 border-garden-500 font-heading font-black text-xl text-garden-800 rounded-2xl transition-all";
        triggerConfetti(clickedBtn);
        window.speak("Super job, Christine! That is correct!");
      } else {
        playSound('incorrect');
        clickedBtn.className = "w-full py-4 px-6 bg-rose-100 border-2 border-rose-500 font-heading font-black text-xl text-rose-800 rounded-2xl transition-all";
        buttons.forEach(btn => {
          if (btn.textContent === q.answer) {
            btn.className = "w-full py-4 px-6 bg-garden-100 border-2 border-garden-500 font-heading font-black text-xl text-garden-800 rounded-2xl transition-all";
          }
        });
        window.speak(`You are so close, Christine! Remember, the correct day is ${q.answer}.`);
      }

      setTimeout(() => {
        dayQuizCurrentIndex++;
        if (dayQuizCurrentIndex < TOTAL_DAY_QUESTIONS) {
          loadDayQuizQuestion();
        } else {
          showDayQuizResults();
        }
      }, 2500);
    }

    function showDayQuizResults() {
      document.getElementById("dayQuizQuestionScreen").classList.add("hidden");
      document.getElementById("dayQuizQuestionScreen").classList.remove("flex");
      document.getElementById("dayQuizResultScreen").classList.remove("hidden");
      document.getElementById("dayQuizResultScreen").classList.add("flex");

      playSound('success');

      const scoreTextEl = document.getElementById("dayQuizFinalScoreText");
      scoreTextEl.textContent = `${dayQuizScore} / 5`;

      const titleEl = document.getElementById("dayQuizFinalTitle");
      const descEl = document.getElementById("dayQuizFinalDescription");

      let msg = "";
      if (dayQuizScore === 5) {
        titleEl.textContent = "🏆 Perfect Score, Christine!";
        descEl.textContent = "Wow Christine! You know all your days and exactly what comes before and after! Buzz is so proud of you!";
        msg = "Wow Christine! You got a perfect score! You know all your days! Buzz is so proud of you!";
      } else {
        titleEl.textContent = "🌟 Terrific Try, Christine!";
        descEl.textContent = `Wonderful! You collected ${dayQuizScore} stars! Let's practice the days of the week together again soon!`;
        msg = `Wonderful Christine! You collected ${dayQuizScore} stars! Let's keep practicing to get all 5 stars next time!`;
      }

      window.speak(msg);
      triggerConfetti(document.getElementById("dayQuizTrophyWrapper"));
    }

    window.restartDayQuiz = function() {
      startDayQuiz();
    };

    // 5. 20-QUESTION ADVENTURE QUIZ ENGINE
    const quizQuestions = [
      { h: 1, m: 0, options: ["1:00", "12:00", "2:00", "6:00"], answer: "1:00", type: "read", text: "Look at the clock! What time is it when the short hand is on 1 and the long hand is on 12?" },
      { h: 7, m: 0, options: ["7:00", "8:00", "12:00", "6:00"], answer: "7:00", type: "read", text: "What time is it on this clock? Hint: The short hand points straight at seven!" },

    window.startQuiz = function() {
      playSound('click');
      quizCurrentIndex = 0;
      quizScore = 0;
      
      document.getElementById("quizStartScreen").classList.add("hidden");
      document.getElementById("quizResultScreen").classList.add("hidden");
      document.getElementById("quizQuestionScreen").classList.remove("hidden");
      document.getElementById("quizQuestionScreen").classList.add("flex");

      buildQuizProgressDots();
      loadQuizQuestion();
    };

    function buildQuizProgressDots() {
      const container = document.getElementById("quizDotsContainer");
      if (!container) return;
      container.innerHTML = "";
      for (let i = 0; i < TOTAL_QUIZ_QUESTIONS; i++) {
        const dot = document.createElement("div");
        dot.className = "w-2 h-2 rounded-full bg-sky-300 transition-all";
        dot.id = `quizDot-${i}`;
        container.appendChild(dot);
      }
    }

    function loadQuizQuestion() {
      const q = quizQuestions[quizCurrentIndex];
      
      document.getElementById("quizCurrentNum").textContent = quizCurrentIndex + 1;
      document.getElementById("quizScoreText").textContent = `Stars: ${quizScore}`;

      for (let i = 0; i < TOTAL_QUIZ_QUESTIONS; i++) {
        const dot = document.getElementById(`quizDot-${i}`);
        if (dot) {
          if (i === quizCurrentIndex) {
            dot.className = "w-3 h-3 rounded-full bg-sky-600 ring-2 ring-white scale-125 transition-all";
          } else if (i < quizCurrentIndex) {
            dot.className = "w-2 h-2 rounded-full bg-garden-500 transition-all";
          } else {
            dot.className = "w-2 h-2 rounded-full bg-sky-300 transition-all";
          }
        }
      }

      document.getElementById("quizQuestionTitle").textContent = q.text;
      updateClockHands(q.h, q.m, "quizHourHand", "quizMinuteHand");

      const container = document.getElementById("quizAnswersContainer");
      container.innerHTML = "";
      q.options.forEach(opt => {
        const btn = document.createElement("button");
        btn.className = "w-full py-3 px-6 bg-slate-100 hover:bg-sky-100 border-2 border-slate-300 hover:border-sky-400 font-heading font-black text-lg text-slate-700 rounded-2xl transition-all transform active:scale-95";
        btn.textContent = opt;
        btn.onclick = () => handleAnswerSubmit(opt, btn);
        container.appendChild(btn);
      });

      setTimeout(() => {
        window.speak(q.text);
      }, 300);
    }

    window.speakQuizQuestion = function() {
      const q = quizQuestions[quizCurrentIndex];
      window.speak(q.text);
    };

    function handleAnswerSubmit(selectedOption, clickedBtn) {
      const q = quizQuestions[quizCurrentIndex];
      const isCorrect = selectedOption === q.answer;

      const container = document.getElementById("quizAnswersContainer");
      const buttons = container.querySelectorAll("button");
      buttons.forEach(btn => btn.disabled = true);

      if (isCorrect) {
        quizScore++;
        playSound('correct');
        clickedBtn.className = "w-full py-3 px-6 bg-garden-100 border-2 border-garden-500 font-heading font-black text-lg text-garden-800 rounded-2xl transition-all";
        triggerConfetti(clickedBtn);
        window.speak("Bzzz! Amazing job! You got it right!");
      } else {
        playSound('incorrect');
        clickedBtn.className = "w-full py-3 px-6 bg-rose-100 border-2 border-rose-500 font-heading font-black text-lg text-rose-800 rounded-2xl transition-all";
        buttons.forEach(btn => {
          if (btn.textContent === q.answer) {
            btn.className = "w-full py-3 px-6 bg-garden-100 border-2 border-garden-500 font-heading font-black text-lg text-garden-800 rounded-2xl transition-all";
          }
        });
        window.speak("Nice try! Buzz says look at the green button for the right answer!");
      }

      setTimeout(() => {
        quizCurrentIndex++;
        if (quizCurrentIndex < TOTAL_QUIZ_QUESTIONS) {
          loadQuizQuestion();
        } else {
          showQuizResults();
        }
      }, 2500);
    }

    function showQuizResults() {
      document.getElementById("quizQuestionScreen").classList.add("hidden");
      document.getElementById("quizQuestionScreen").classList.remove("flex");
      document.getElementById("quizResultScreen").classList.remove("hidden");
      document.getElementById("quizResultScreen").classList.add("flex");

      playSound('success');

      const scorePct = (quizScore / TOTAL_QUIZ_QUESTIONS) * 100;
      const titleEl = document.getElementById("quizFinalTitle");
      const descEl = document.getElementById("quizFinalDescription");
      const scoreTextEl = document.getElementById("quizFinalScoreText");

      scoreTextEl.textContent = `${quizScore} / ${TOTAL_QUIZ_QUESTIONS}`;

      let voiceMessage = "";
      if (scorePct === 100) {
        titleEl.textContent = "🏆 Perfect Clock Master!";
        descEl.textContent = "Unbelievable! You answered every single question correctly! You are officially a Clock Master! Buzz is so proud of you, Christine!";
        voiceMessage = "Unbelievable, Christine! You got a perfect score! You are officially a clock master! Buzz is so proud of you!";
      } else if (scorePct >= 70) {
        titleEl.textContent = "🌟 Stellar Explorer!";
        descEl.textContent = `Wonderful job, Christine! You collected ${quizScore} stars! You are extremely good at reading clocks. Let's practice a bit more to get 20!`;
        voiceMessage = `Wonderful job, Christine! You collected ${quizScore} stars! You are extremely good at reading clocks. Let's practice a bit more next time!`;
      } else {
        titleEl.textContent = "⏰ Clock Apprentice!";
        descEl.textContent = `Great effort, Christine! You collected ${quizScore} stars. Let's read the learning secrets again and try one more time!`;
        voiceMessage = `Great effort, Christine! You collected ${quizScore} stars. Let's read the learning secrets again and try one more time to collect more stars!`;
      }

      window.speak(voiceMessage);
      triggerConfetti(document.getElementById("quizTrophyWrapper"));
    }

    window.restartQuiz = function() {
      startQuiz();
    };

    function triggerConfetti(targetEl) {
      if (!targetEl) return;
      const rect = targetEl.getBoundingClientRect();
      const parent = document.body;
      const colors = ['#f59e0b', '#38bdf8', '#22c55e', '#f43f5e', '#a855f7'];

      for (let i = 0; i < 30; i++) {
        const confetti = document.createElement("div");
        confetti.className = "confetti";
        confetti.style.backgroundColor = colors[Math.floor(Math.random() * colors.length)];
        confetti.style.left = `${rect.left + window.scrollX + rect.width / 2 + (Math.random() - 0.5) * 60}px`;
        confetti.style.top = `${rect.top + window.scrollY + rect.height / 2 + (Math.random() - 0.5) * 40}px`;
        
        const angle = Math.random() * Math.PI * 2;
        const distance = Math.random() * 80 + 40;
        const destX = Math.cos(angle) * distance;
        const destY = Math.sin(angle) * distance;

        confetti.style.setProperty('--dest-x', `${destX}px`);
        confetti.style.setProperty('--dest-y', `${destY}px`);
        
        confetti.style.animationDuration = `${0.6 + Math.random() * 0.8}s`;
        parent.appendChild(confetti);

        setTimeout(() => {
          confetti.remove();
        }, 1500);
      }
    }

    // 6. REUSABLE LEGAL MODAL
    window.openLegal = function(title, bodyHtml) {
      playSound('click');
      document.getElementById("legalTitle").textContent = title;
      document.getElementById("legalBody").innerHTML = bodyHtml;
      document.getElementById("legalModal").style.display = "flex";
    };

    window.closeLegal = function() {
      playSound('click');
      document.getElementById("legalModal").style.display = "none";
    };

    // 7. INITIALIZATION ON LOAD
    generateMinuteTicks();
    updateClockHands(sandboxHour, sandboxMinute, "hourHand", "minuteHand");
    window.selectDay(2); // Initialize to Tuesday as default
    document.getElementById("currentYear").textContent = new Date().getFullYear();

    const _io = new IntersectionObserver(entries => {
      entries.forEach(e => {
        if (e.isIntersecting) {
          e.target.classList.add('visible');
          _io.unobserve(e.target);
        }
      });
    }, { threshold: 0.08, rootMargin: '0px 0px -40px 0px' });
    
    document.querySelectorAll('.reveal').forEach(el => _io.observe(el));
    
    setTimeout(function() {
      document.querySelectorAll('.reveal').forEach(function(el) {
        el.style.opacity = '1';
        el.style.transform = 'none';
      });
    }, 1000);

    document.querySelectorAll('a[href^="#"]').forEach(a => {
      a.addEventListener('click', function(e) {
        e.preventDefault();
        const targetId = this.getAttribute('href');
        if (targetId === '#') return;
        const el = document.querySelector(targetId);
        if (el) {
          window.scrollTo({
            top: el.getBoundingClientRect().top + window.pageYOffset - 90,
            behavior: 'smooth'
          });
        }
      });
    });

  })();
  </script>

  <!-- Auto-initialize Lucide Icons -->
  <script>
    lucide.createIcons();
  </script>

</body>
</html>
